(function(){
  function el(id){return document.getElementById(id);}
  function hideLac(){var p=el('lacPanel');if(p)p.style.display='none';}
  window.lacSwitchTab=function(which){
    var d=el('lacDataView'),p=el('lacProductView'),dt=el('lacDataTab'),pt=el('lacProductTab');
    var isProduct=which==='product';
    if(d)d.style.display=isProduct?'none':'block';if(p)p.style.display=isProduct?'block':'none';
    if(dt)dt.className='lacTab'+(isProduct?'':' active');if(pt)pt.className='lacTab'+(isProduct?' active':'');
  };
  function lacToday(){var d=new Date();return ('0'+d.getDate()).slice(-2)+'/'+('0'+(d.getMonth()+1)).slice(-2)+'/'+d.getFullYear();}
  function currentClientName(){
    var n=el('clientDbName'),s=el('clientDbSurname'),a=[],v;
    if(s&&(v=String(s.value||'').replace(/^\s+|\s+$/g,'')))a.push(v);
    if(n&&(v=String(n.value||'').replace(/^\s+|\s+$/g,'')))a.push(v);
    return a.join(' ');
  }
  window.openLacDevice=function(){
    var ids=['dashboardPanel','analysisPanel','prescriptionPanel','visualExamPanel','indicationsPanel','hearingPanel','clientsPanel','analysisTabs'];
    for(var i=0;i<ids.length;i++){var x=el(ids[i]);if(x)x.style.display='none';}
    var report=el('reportSectionTop'),nav=el('moduleNav'),p=el('lacPanel');
    if(report)report.style.display='none';if(nav)nav.style.display='flex';if(p)p.style.display='block';
    try{if(typeof cloudNavActive==='function')cloudNavActive('');}catch(e){}
    var name=el('lacPatientName'),date=el('lacDate');if(name&&!name.value)name.value=currentClientName();if(date&&!date.value)date.value=lacToday();
    lacSwitchTab('data');try{window.scrollTo(0,0);}catch(e2){}
  };
  window.lacResetData=function(){
    var root=el('lacDataView');if(!root)return;if(!confirm('Azzera tutti i dati della scheda LAC?'))return;
    var fields=root.querySelectorAll('input,textarea,select');for(var i=0;i<fields.length;i++){if(fields[i].type==='checkbox'||fields[i].type==='radio')fields[i].checked=false;else fields[i].value='';}
    var date=el('lacDate');if(date)date.value=lacToday();var name=el('lacPatientName');if(name)name.value=currentClientName();
  };
  if(typeof window.showDashboard==='function'){var oldDash=window.showDashboard;window.showDashboard=function(){hideLac();return oldDash.apply(this,arguments);};}
  if(typeof window.showModule==='function'){var oldMod=window.showModule;window.showModule=function(){hideLac();return oldMod.apply(this,arguments);};}
  if(typeof window.dashboardNewClient==='function'){var oldNew=window.dashboardNewClient;window.dashboardNewClient=function(){hideLac();return oldNew.apply(this,arguments);};}
  if(typeof window.clientSidebarOpenSection==='function'){var oldSide=window.clientSidebarOpenSection;window.clientSidebarOpenSection=function(){hideLac();return oldSide.apply(this,arguments);};}
})();

  function fieldLabel(f){var root=e('lacDataView'),lab=null,label='';if(f.id&&root){try{lab=root.querySelector('label[for="'+f.id.replace(/\"/g,'')+'"]');}catch(z){}}if(lab)label=(lab.textContent||'').trim();if(!label)label=(f.getAttribute('aria-label')||'').trim();if(!label&&(f.type==='checkbox'||f.type==='radio')){var card=f.closest?f.closest('.lacParamCard'):null,head=card?card.querySelector('.lacParamHead'):null,parent=f.parentNode;label=((head?(head.textContent||'').trim()+' ':'')+(parent?(parent.textContent||'').trim():'')).trim();}return label||'Dato';}
  function collectData(){var root=e('lacDataView'),out=[];if(!root)return out;var fs=root.querySelectorAll('input,textarea,select');for(var i=0;i<fs.length;i++){var f=fs[i],typ=(f.type||'').toLowerCase(),val='';if(typ==='checkbox'||typ==='radio'){if(!f.checked)continue;val='Selezionato';}else{if(String(f.tagName||'').toUpperCase()==='SELECT'&&f.selectedIndex>=0)val=String((f.options[f.selectedIndex].textContent||f.value)||'').trim();else val=String(f.value||'').trim();if(!val)continue;}out.push({label:fieldLabel(f),value:val});}return out;}
  function rowHtml(x){return '<div class="lacSummaryRow"><div class="lacSummaryRowLabel">'+esc(x.label)+'</div><div class="lacSummaryRowValue">'+esc(x.value)+'</div></div>';}

(function(){
  var active={OD:1,OS:1};

  function parseAxis(v){
    if(v===null||v===undefined||String(v).trim()==='')return null;
    var n=parseFloat(String(v).replace(',','.'));
    if(!isFinite(n))return null;
    n=Math.abs(n);
    if(n>180)n=180;
    return Math.max(0,Math.min(180,n));
  }

  function physicalForValue(eye,v){return international(eye)?180-v:v;}
  function valueForPhysical(eye,p){return international(eye)?180-p:p;}
  function pt(angle,r){var rad=angle*Math.PI/180;return{x:180+r*Math.cos(rad),y:150-r*Math.sin(rad)};}

  function gonioMarkup(eye,value){
    var svg='<svg viewBox="0 0 360 190" role="img" aria-label="Goniometro '+eye+'">';
    svg+='<path d="M 30 150 A 150 150 0 0 1 330 150" fill="none" stroke="#425667" stroke-width="2"/><line x1="24" y1="150" x2="336" y2="150" stroke="#425667" stroke-width="1.7"/>';
    var d,a,b,label;
    for(d=0;d<=180;d+=5){a=pt(d,150);b=pt(d,150-(d%10===0?13:7));svg+='<line x1="'+a.x.toFixed(1)+'" y1="'+a.y.toFixed(1)+'" x2="'+b.x.toFixed(1)+'" y2="'+b.y.toFixed(1)+'" stroke="#758696" stroke-width="1"/>';}
    for(d=0;d<=180;d+=10){a=pt(d,124);label=international(eye)?180-d:d;svg+='<text x="'+a.x.toFixed(1)+'" y="'+(a.y+3).toFixed(1)+'" text-anchor="middle" font-size="9" font-family="Segoe UI,Arial" fill="#536b7d">'+label+'</text>';}
    svg+='<circle cx="180" cy="150" r="7" fill="#fff" stroke="#425667" stroke-width="1.5"/>';
    if(value!==null){var phys=physicalForValue(eye,value);a=pt(phys,112);svg+='<line x1="180" y1="150" x2="'+a.x.toFixed(1)+'" y2="'+a.y.toFixed(1)+'" stroke="#1769aa" stroke-width="4" stroke-linecap="round"/><circle cx="'+a.x.toFixed(1)+'" cy="'+a.y.toFixed(1)+'" r="4" fill="#1769aa"/><text x="180" y="185" text-anchor="middle" font-size="12" font-weight="800" font-family="Segoe UI,Arial" fill="#1769aa">AX '+Math.round(value)+'° · '+(international(eye)?'INTERNAZIONALE':'TABO')+'</text>';}
    else svg+='<text x="180" y="185" text-anchor="middle" font-size="11" font-weight="700" font-family="Segoe UI,Arial" fill="#6b7f90">AX — · '+(international(eye)?'INTERNAZIONALE':'TABO')+'</text>';
    return svg+'</svg>';
  }
  function currentValue(eye){var inp=axisInput(eye,active[eye]||1);return inp?parseAxis(inp.value):null;}
  function highlight(eye){for(var r=1;r<=4;r++){var inp=axisInput(eye,r);if(inp)inp.classList.toggle('lacAxisActiveInput',r===active[eye]);}}
  function render(eye){var box=g('lacAxisGonio'+eye),st=g('lacAxisStatus'+eye),v=currentValue(eye);if(box)box.innerHTML=gonioMarkup(eye,v);highlight(eye);if(st){if(v===null){st.className='lacAxisStatus';st.textContent='Seleziona una casella AX '+eye+' e inserisci un valore da 0° a 180°.';}else{st.className='lacAxisStatus hasValue';st.textContent='AX riga '+active[eye]+': '+Math.round(v)+'° · '+(international(eye)?'Sistema Internazionale':'Sistema TABO');}}var mir=g('lacAxisModeOSMirror');if(mir)mir.textContent=osMode()==='international'?'INTERNAZIONALE':'TABO';}
  window.lacRenderAxes=function(){rend

  window.lacProductRecalc=function(){var od=productById(e('lacOdProduct')?e('lacOdProduct').value:''),os=productById(e('lacOsProduct')?e('lacOsProduct').value:'');var op=od?od.price:0,sp=os?os.price:0;if(e('lacOdPrice'))e('lacOdPrice').innerHTML=eur(op);if(e('lacOsPrice'))e('lacOsPrice').innerHTML=eur(sp);if(e('lacOdCost'))e('lacOdCost').value=od?Number(op).toFixed(2).replace('.',','):'';if(e('lacOsCost'))e('lacOsCost').value=os?Number(sp).toFixed(2).replace('.',','):'';if(typeof window.lacProductCostChanged==='function')window.lacProductCostChanged();else if(e('lacProductTotal'))e('lacProductTotal').innerHTML=eur(op+sp);};
})();

<script id="optykerOnlineOrdersScript">
(function(){
  var shopState={tab:'new',orders:[],shopifyClients:[],loading:false};
  var SHOPIFY_ADMIN_STORE='vkk3n8-d0';
  function e(id){return document.getElementById(id);}
  function h(v){return (window.escapeHtml?escapeHtml(String(v==null?'':v)):String(v==null?'':v).replace(/[&<>\"]/g,function(c){return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[c];}));}
  function money(v,c){var n=parseFloat(v||0);if(isNaN(n))n=0;try{return new Intl.NumberFormat('it-IT',{style:'currency',currency:c||'EUR'}).format(n);}catch(z){return '€ '+n.toFixed(2).replace('.',',');}}
  function dt(v){if(!v)return '';try{return new Date(v).toLocaleString('it-IT',{day:'2-digit',month:'2-digit',year:'numeric',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});}catch(z){return String(v);}}

function downloadVisualExam(){
  var html=visualExamExportHtml();
  if(!html){ alert('Scheda non disponibile.'); return; }
  var exam=VISUAL_EXAMS[currentVisualExam];
  saveHtmlDocument(html,'Esame_Visivo_'+cleanFileNamePart(exam.name)+exportPatientSuffix());
}
function printVisualExam(){
  var html=visualExamExportHtml();
  if(!html){ alert('Scheda non disponibile.'); return; }
  printHtmlDocument(html);
}
function getPrintFrame(){
  var frame = g('printFrame');
  if(!frame){
    frame = document.createElement('iframe');
    frame.id = 'printFrame';
    frame.style.position = 'absolute';
    frame.style.left = '-10000px';
    frame.style.top = '0';
    frame.style.width = '1px';
    frame.style.height = '1px';
    document.body.appendChild(frame);
  }
  return frame;
}
function getIePageSetupState(){
  var state = {shell:null, header:null, footer:null, ok:false};
  try{
    var shell = new ActiveXObject('WScript.Shell');
    state.shell = shell;
    try{ state.header = shell.RegRead('HKCU\\Software\\Microsoft\\Internet Explorer\\PageSetup\\header'); }catch(e1){ state.header = null; }
    try{ state.footer = shell.RegRead('HKCU\\Software\\Microsoft\\Internet Explorer\\PageSetup\\footer'); }catch(e2){ state.footer = null; }
    shell.RegWrite('HKCU\\Software\\Microsoft\\Internet Explorer\\PageSetup\\header', '', 'REG_SZ');
    shell.RegWrite('HKCU\\Software\\Microsoft\\Internet Explorer\\PageSetup\\footer', '', 'REG_SZ');
    state.ok = true;
  }catch(e){ state.ok = false; }
  return state;
}
function restoreIePageSetupState(state){
  if(!state || !state.ok || !state.shell) return;
  try{
    if(state.header !== null) state.shell.RegWrite('HKCU\\Software\\Microsoft\\Internet Explorer\\PageSetup\\header', state.header, 'REG_SZ');
    else state.shell.RegWrite('HKCU\\Software\\Microsoft\\Internet Explorer\\PageSetup\\header', '', 'REG_SZ');
  }catch(e1){}
  try{
    if(state.footer !== null) state.shell.RegWrite('HKCU\\Software\\Microsoft\\Internet Explorer\\PageSetup\\footer', state.footer, 'REG_SZ');
    else state.shell.RegWrite('HKCU\\Software\\Microsoft\\Internet Explorer\\PageSetup\\footer', '', 'REG_SZ');
  }catch(e2){}
}
function printHtmlDocument(html,delayMs){
  try{
    var frame = getPrintFrame();
    var doc = frame.contentWindow.document;
    var delay=parseInt(delayMs,10);
    if(isNaN(delay) || delay<100) delay=350;
    doc.open();
    doc.write(html);
    doc.close();
    frame.contentWindow.focus();
    setTimeout(function(){
      var pageSetup = getIePageSetupState();
      try{
        frame.contentWindow.print();
      }catch(err){
        alert('Errore di stampa: ' + err.message);
      }finally{
        restoreIePageSetupState(pageSetup);
      }
    }, delay);
  }catch(e){
    alert('Errore di stampa: ' + e.message);
  }
}

  updatePrescriptionLinkedData();
  refreshPrescriptionGoniometers();
  var frame = getPrintFrame();
  frame.style.width = '794px';
  frame.style.height = '1123px';
  printHtmlDocument(prescriptionPrintHtml());
}

    if(v===null||v===undefined||String(v).trim()==='')return null;
    var n=parseFloat(String(v).replace(',','.'));
    if(!isFinite(n))return null;
    n=Math.abs(n);
    if(n>180)n=180;
    return Math.max(0,Math.min(180,n));

  function valueForPhysical(eye,p){return international(eye)?180-p:p;}

function cloudLoadClients(){
  if(OPTYKER_CLOUD.loadingClients || !OPTYKER_CLOUD.username) return Promise.resolve(OPTYKER_CLOUD.clients);
  OPTYKER_CLOUD.loadingClients=true; cloudSetStatus('Sincronizzazione clienti in corso…',true);
  return cloudApi('list_clients',{}).then(function(x){ OPTYKER_CLOUD.clients=(x.data||[]).map(cloudDbToClient); cloudSetStatus('Cloud collegato · '+OPTYKER_CLOUD.clients.length+' clienti sincronizzati',true); clientRefreshList(); dashboardRenderClients(); return OPTYKER_CLOUD.clients; }).catch(function(e){ cloudSetStatus('Cloud non raggiungibile: '+e.message,false); throw e; }).finally(function(){OPTYKER_CLOUD.loadingClients=false;});
}
function cloudLoadSheets(clientId){ if(!clientId) return Promise.resolve([]); return cloudApi('list_sheets',{client_id:clientId}).then(function(x){ OPTYKER_CLOUD.sheets[clientId]=x.data||[]; return OPTYKER_CLOUD.sheets[clientId]; }); }
function cloudLoadConsents(clientId){ if(!clientId) return Promise.resolve([]); return cloudApi('list_consents',{client_id:clientId}).then(function(x){ OPTYKER_CLOUD.consents[clientId]=x.data||[]; return OPTYKER_CLOUD.consents[clientId]; }); }
function cloudFindClient(id){ var i; for(i=0;i<OPTYKER_CLOUD.clients.length;i++) if(OPTYKER_CLOUD.clients[i].id===id) return OPTYKER_CLOUD.clients[i]; return null; }

  function renderOrders(){
    var h=e('clientOnlineOrdersFull');if(!h)return;
    if(!SP.clientId){h.innerHTML='<div class="clientCommerceEmpty">Seleziona un cliente.</div>';return;}
    if(!SP.orders.length){h.innerHTML='<div class="clientCommerceEmpty">Nessun ordine online collegato a questo cliente.</div>';return;}
    h.innerHTML=SP.orders.map(function(o){
      var d=o.data||{},items=Array.isArray(d.items)?d.items:[],products='';
      if(items.length)products=items.map(function(it){var title=it.title||it.name||'Prodotto',qty=Number(it.quantity)||1,v=it.variant_title||it.variantTitle||'';return hh(qty+' × '+title+(v?' · '+v:''));}).join('<br>');
      return '<div class="clientCommerceItem"><div class="clientCommerceTop"><div><div class="clientCommerceName">'+hh(o.order_name||'Ordine online')+'</div><div class="clientCommerceMeta">'+hh(dt(o.order_date))+' · '+hh((o.financial_status||'').toUpperCase())+' · '+hh((o.fulfillment_status||'').toUpperCase())+'</div></div><div class="clientCommercePrice">'+hh(eur(o.total,o.currency))+'</div></div>'+(products?'<div class="clientOnlineOrderProducts">'+products+'</div>':'')+'<div class="clientOnlineOrderActions"><button class="secondary" type="button" onclick="clientOpenOnlineOrder(\''+hh(o.order_name||'')+'\')">Apri nella gestione ordini</button></div></div>';
    }).join('');
  }

function clientDeleteCurrent(){
  if(!clientCurrentId){ alert('Seleziona prima un cliente da eliminare.'); return; }
  var meta=clientReadJson(clientMetaPath(clientCurrentId))||clientMetadataFromForm();
  var name=trimText(((meta.surname||'')+' '+(meta.name||'')));
  if(!confirm('Eliminare definitivamente il cliente'+(name?' '+name:'')+' e tutte le sue visite?')) return;
  try{

function clientOnlineOrderCount(){
  if(!clientCurrentId)return 0;
  var map=window.OPTYKER_CLIENT_ONLINE_ORDER_COUNTS||{};
  if(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(map,clientCurrentId))return Number(map[clientCurrentId])||0;
  var c=(typeof cloudFindClient==='function')?cloudFindClient(clientCurrentId):null;
  return Number(c&&(c.shopifyOrdersCount!=null?c.shopifyOrdersCount:c.shopify_orders_count))||0;
}

function clientSidebarRender(){
  var sub=g('clientSidebarSubmenu');if(!sub)return;
  if(!clientCurrentId){sub.className='clientSidebarSubmenu';sub.innerHTML='';return;}
  var x=clientSidebarTypes(),h='',i,t,rows,j;
  h+='<button class="clientSideSubBtn'+(clientWorkspaceSection==='anagrafica'?' active':'')+'" type="button" onclick="clientSidebarOpenSection(\'anagrafica\')"><span>Anagrafica</span></button>';
  var onlineCount=clientOnlineOrderCount();
  if(onlineCount>0)h+='<button class="clientSideSubBtn'+(clientWorkspaceSection==='onlineorders'?' active':'')+'" type="button" onclick="clientSidebarOpenOnlineOrders()"><span>Ordini online</span><span class="sideCount">'+onlineCount+'</span></button>';
  for(i=0;i<x.types.length;i++){
    t=x.types[i];if(!x.counts[t])continue;
    h+='<button class="clientSideSubBtn'+(clientWorkspaceSection===t?' active':'')+'" type="button" onclick="clientSidebarOpenSection(\''+String(t).replace(/'/g,"\\'")+'\')"><span>'+escapeHtml(clientWorkspaceTypeLabel(t))+'</span><span class="sideCount">'+x.counts[t]+'</span></button>';
    if(clientWorkspaceSection===t){
      rows=clientRowsForSection(t);h+='<div class="clientSideDates">';
      if(!rows.length)h+='<div class="clientSideEmpty">Nessuna data</div>';
      for(j=0;j<rows.length;j++)h+='<button class="clientSideDateBtn'+(rows[j].id===clientWorkspaceSheetId?' active':'')+'" type="button" onclick="clientSidebarOpenDate(\''+String(t).replace(/'/g,"\\'")+'\',\''+rows[j].id+'\')">'+escapeHtml(clientSheetDateOnly(rows[j]))+'</button>';
      h+='</div>';
    }
  }
  sub.innerHTML=h;sub.className='clientSidebarSubmenu visible';
}

    if(clientWorkspaceSection===t){
      rows=clientRowsForSection(t);h+='<div class="clientSideDates">';
      if(!rows.length)h+='<div class="clientSideEmpty">Nessuna data</div>';
      for(j=0;j<rows.length;j++)h+='<button class="clientSideDateBtn'+(rows[j].id===clientWorkspaceSheetId?' active':'')+'" type="button" onclick="clientSidebarOpenDate(\''+String(t).replace(/'/g,"\\'")+'\',\''+rows[j].id+'\')">'+escapeHtml(clientSheetDateOnly(rows[j]))+'</button>';
      h+='</div>';
    }
  }
  sub.innerHTML=h;sub.className='clientSidebarSubmenu visible';
}

<script id="OPTYKER_PRINT_WAIT_FIX_V1">
(function(){
  window.printHtmlDocument=function(html,delayMs){
    try{
      var frame=getPrintFrame();
      var doc=frame.contentWindow.document;
      var delay=parseInt(delayMs,10);
      if(isNaN(delay)||delay<100)delay=350;
      doc.open();doc.write(html);doc.close();
      var printed=false;
      function doPrint(){
        if(printed)return;printed=true;
        frame.contentWindow.focus();
        setTimeout(function(){
          var pageSetup=getIePageSetupState();
          try{frame.contentWindow.print();}
          catch(err){alert('Errore di stampa: '+err.message);}
          finally{restoreIePageSetupState(pageSetup);}
        },delay);
      }
      var imgs=doc.images?Array.prototype.slice.call(doc.images):[];
      if(!imgs.length){doPrint();return;}
      var pending=imgs.length;
      function done(){pending--;if(pending<=0)doPrint();}
      for(var i=0;i<imgs.length;i++){
        var im=imgs[i];
        if(im.complete&&im.naturalWidth>0)done();
        else{im.addEventListener('load',done,{once:true});im.addEventListener('error',done,{once:true});}
      }
      setTimeout(doPrint,2500);
    }catch(e){alert('Errore di stampa: '+e.message);}
  };
})();
</script>

function showModule(which){
  var dashboard = g('dashboardPanel');
  if(dashboard) dashboard.style.display='none';
  dashboardSetWorkAreaVisible(true);
  var analysis = g('analysisPanel');
  var prescription = g('prescriptionPanel');
  var visual = g('visualExamPanel');
  var indications = g('indicationsPanel');
  var hearing = g('hearingPanel');
  var clients = g('clientsPanel');
  var tabs = g('analysisTabs');
  var aBtn = g('navAnalysis');
  var pBtn = g('navPrescription');
  var vBtn = g('navVisualExam');
  var iBtn = g('navIndications');
  var hBtn = g('navHearing');
  var cBtn = g('navClients');
  analysis.style.display='none';
  prescription.style.display='none';
  if(visual) visual.style.display='none';
  if(indications) indications.style.display='none';
  if(hearing) hearing.style.display='none';
  if(clients) clients.style.display='none';
  if(tabs) tabs.style.display='none';
  aBtn.className='moduleBtn';
  pBtn.className='moduleBtn';
  if(vBtn) vBtn.className='moduleBtn';
  if(iBtn) iBtn.className='moduleBtn';
  if(hBtn) hBtn.className='moduleBtn';
  if(cBtn) cBtn.className='moduleBtn';
  if(which==='prescription'){
    prescription.style.display='block';
    pBtn.className='moduleBtn active';
    updatePrescriptionLinkedData();
  } else if(which==='visualexam'){
    if(visual) visual.style.display='block';
    if(vBtn) vBtn.className='moduleBtn active';
    updateVisualExamPatient();
    renderVisualExam();
  } else if(which==='indications'){
    if(indications) indications.style.display='block';
    if(iBtn) iBtn.className='moduleBtn active';
    updatePrescriptionSpecialist();
    renderIndicationsUse();
  } else if(which==='hearing'){
    if(hearing) hearing.style.display='block';
    if(hBtn) hBtn.className='moduleBtn active';
    updateHearingPatientFromActiveClient(false);
    drawHearingAudiogram();
    renderHearingImage();
  } else if(which==='clients'){
    if(clients) clients.style.display='block';
    if(cBtn) cBtn.className='moduleBtn active';
    clientShowView('archive');
    clientRefreshList();
    clientRenderVisits();
  } else {
    analysis.style.display='block';
    if(tabs) tabs.style.display='flex';
    aBtn.className='moduleBtn active';
  }
}
function updatePrescriptionSpecialist(){
  var name=reportFieldValue('specialistName');
  if(g('rxSpecialistFooter')) g('rxSpecialistFooter').innerHTML=escapeHtml(name || 'Michael Mologni');
  if(g('iuSpecialistFooter')) g('iuSpecialistFooter').innerHTML=escapeHtml(name || 'Michael Mologni');
}
function getPatientName(){ return reportFieldValue('clientName'); }
function getPatientSurname(){ return reportFieldValue('clientSurname'); }
function getPatientFullName(){
  var nm = trimText(getPatientName());
  var sn = trimText(getPatientSurname());
  return trimText((nm ? nm : '') + (sn ? ' ' + sn : ''));
}
function updatePrescriptionLinkedData(){
  updatePrescriptionSpecialist();
  updateRxPdOO();
}
function copyReportDataToPrescription(){
  updatePrescriptionLinkedData();
  refreshPrescriptionGoniometers();
}
function rxVal(id){ var e=g(id); return e?trimText(e.value):''; }
function parseRxNumber(v){
  var t=trimText(v||'').replace(',', '.');
  if(!t) return null;
  var n=parseFloat(t);
  return isNaN(n) ? null : n;
}
function formatRxNumber(n){
  if(n===null || isNaN(n)) return '';
  var rounded=Math.round(n*100)/100;
  return String(rounded).replace('.', ',');
}
function updateRxPdSum(odId,osId,ooId){
  var od=parseRxNumber(rxVal(odId));
  var os=parseRxNumber(rxVal(osId));
  var oo=g(ooId);
  if(!oo) return;
  oo.value=(od===null || os===null) ? '' : formatRxNumber(od+os);
}
function updateRxPdOO(){
  updateRxPdSum('rxPdOD','rxPdOS','rxPdOO');
  updateRxPdSum('rxPdNearOD','rxPdNearOS','rxPdNearOO');
}
function rxPrismPrint(id){
  var v=rxVal(id);
  return v ? escapeHtml(v)+'&#916;' : '';
}
function rxChecked(id){ var e=g(id); return !!(e && e.checked); }
function rxCheckMark(id){ return rxChecked(id) ? '&#9745;' : '&#9744;'; }

  if(v===null || v===undefined) return null;
